# Stage15 — complete 230-type reference-setting catalogue

Status: owner-requested planning/reference data, generated 2026-11-04. This is
an actual complete finite representative list for every type 1–230. The
[Stage15 plan](review.md) owns the proposed runtime contract; this catalogue
selects neither a Hotpot dependency nor a production release.

## Meaning, setting and exact format

Every `xyz` expression below is one exact affine operation in the notation of
[the operation table](operation_table.md): `f↦R f+t`, fractional column vectors.
The data file stores every actual operator as well as that expression; it is
not a list of generators. The half-open representatives describe `G/T_cell`,
where `T_cell={T_n:n∈Z³}` for the selected conventional cell. Reconstruct the
infinite group as **all** `T_n r_i`; retain every integer carry in algebra.
The full Bravais translation subgroup `T` also contains the listed centring
translations. Thus `G/T` is the point group, and a centred conventional-cell
representative list is larger than its point group.

The setting is the IUCr reference setting: monoclinic unique axis **b**, cell
choice **1**; origin choice **2** where two origin choices exist; rhombohedral
groups use **hexagonal axes** (`:h`). The remaining axes and origins are fixed
by the exact Hall symbol and operators, rather than an unstated name default.
Every entry is already in its reference frame, so `f_reference=I f+0`.
`Hall origin` in the index means exactly the origin in that Hall/xyz definition;
it does not assert an inversion centre for a noncentrosymmetric group.

**230 types does not mean all settings.** Alternate monoclinic axes/cell choices,
origin 1, rhombohedral primitive axes, unconventional cells and other Hall
alternatives are not extra types in this list. Importers must resolve their
actual setting and apply an explicit exact basis/origin/lattice transformation
under the SpaceGroup contract, or retain an identified unavailable projection.
Never treat matching type numbers as matching settings. The proposed group
transformation must recompute the new cell quotient when the translation index
changes; reusing only the old finite list misses or duplicates operators.
Magnetic space groups, superspace, layer and rod groups are outside this
ordinary 3D catalogue, not silently mapped to one of these 230 types.

[Exact machine-readable data](space_group_catalogue.json) schema
`hotpot-stage15-space-group-catalogue/1`:

- `rotations[k]` is the row-major integer 3×3 `R`.
- Each `operations[i]=[k,[u,v,w],xyz]` is exactly `R=rotations[k]`,
  `t=(u,v,w)/24`; the denominator 24 is a catalogue encoding only. Hotpot's
  proposed general rational algebra is not restricted to it.
- `centering_t24` enumerates **all** pure-translation representatives,
  including zero. `T=⋃_c(Z³+c/24)` reconstructs the full translation lattice.
- `cell_order` counts conventional-cell representatives; `point_group_order`
  counts distinct `R`. Neither is the cardinality of the infinite group.
- Ordering is identity first, then exact lexicographic `(R,t)`; each `rNNN`
  in a block is the zero-based operation index in that group's data entry.
- `hermann_mauguin` uses COMCIFS's current reference names; the exact Gemmi
  legacy label is separately retained as `source_hermann_mauguin`. In particular
  types 39/41/64/67/68 use `e`-glide names without changing their operators.

Across these **230 selected reference settings**, the 4,425 listed operation
occurrences contain **786 distinct exact `(R,t)` pairs**, with **64** distinct
`R` and **56** distinct reduced `t`. These counts use the selected fractional
reference-cell coordinates; they are not setting-independent invariants or
counts of the infinite groups. This cross-group union is a catalogue union,
not one closed crystallographic group. The counted numeric pairs omit group
context; proposed SymmUnit equality also includes its immutable group/setting.

The index links directly to the **full** lists below. Open only the needed
entry; the main implementation plan need not load this entire catalogue.

## Provenance and reproducibility

Operators were generated by a small standalone C++ extractor using only official
Gemmi **0.7.5**, commit `5cc1c23c6007e0e6cbd69289c6f7c0bff50e943e`,
`get_spacegroup_reference_setting(n).operations().all_ops_sorted()` for every
`n=1…230`, then reordered and encoded exactly. `find_spacegroup_by_number` is
not the selector: e.g. type 48 has origin-choice 1 as a table-first alternative.
All 230 selected Gemmi entries have identity reference-basis transform and occur
within the first 530 standard table entries. No OpenBabel/RDKit/CCDC Python API,
Hotpot import, package install or global configuration change is involved.

- [Official pinned symmetry table and Hall generator](https://github.com/project-gemmi/gemmi/blob/5cc1c23c6007e0e6cbd69289c6f7c0bff50e943e/src/symmetry.cpp),
  SHA256 `fae1e4e6f297bbca5ff22126e5f99a54b60d8bcda0f003256558d2bb6426489c`.
- [Official pinned symmetry header](https://github.com/project-gemmi/gemmi/blob/5cc1c23c6007e0e6cbd69289c6f7c0bff50e943e/include/gemmi/symmetry.hpp),
  SHA256 `380b2f2d9107ff5f798b0b0881ae28883d33be58074923e24a7c4830dea89ed1`.
- [Official source documentation](https://github.com/project-gemmi/gemmi/blob/5cc1c23c6007e0e6cbd69289c6f7c0bff50e943e/docs/symmetry.rst)
  records its upstream CCP4/sgtbx provenance and reference-setting/name choices.
  The table and its Hall generator are **one source route**, not two independent
  scientific oracles.
- Current reference labels and Hall metadata were independently retrieved from
  [COMCIFS Enumeration Templates 1.4.9](https://github.com/COMCIFS/Enumeration_Templates/blob/7bd3b60895b20f4e9db22c169d1970767b1bb491/templ_enum.cif),
  commit `7bd3b60895b20f4e9db22c169d1970767b1bb491`,
  SHA256 `5b224ae7d4e360e049459644cbe662a7995fb2d4213b313bff94e5d137959184`,
  `save_ref_set`. This comparison has separate provenance but shared historical
  crystallographic-table ancestry; it is not an independent all-operator oracle.
- [COMCIFS core dictionary](https://github.com/COMCIFS/cif_core/blob/93b39f69529f489815dd1020376ce1df267f2eee/cif_core.dic),
  commit `93b39f69529f489815dd1020376ce1df267f2eee`,
  SHA256 `9036f4aea461bcb47a1445cd287e71431c8a98ff25fa74057dfd8a60272dfa73`,
  documents exact operation notation, complete representatives, reference choices
  and an independently described off-origin glide.
- Source notices: Copyright Global Phasing Ltd., Gemmi MPL-2.0
  ([preserved licence](space_group_catalogue.GEMMI-LICENSE.txt)); COMCIFS
  enumeration metadata CC-BY-4.0, extracted/reformatted/combined here with
  generated operators ([licence text](space_group_catalogue.COMCIFS-LICENSE.txt)).
  Raw upstream files, source hashes and the generator are in the evidence index.
  This is a versioned documentation derivation, not an unmaintained hand-copied
  runtime table. Runtime lookup/distribution remains a separate 15A decision.

Actual checks and coverage limits are recorded in the Stage15 evidence index
and the plan's evidence paragraph. No catalogue check establishes molecular,
metric-tolerance, CIF, installed-package or Crystal runtime acceptance.
